## Recommendations (recommendations only — nothing changed; approval in-thread)

**No tuning lever is proposed this cycle.** Every active axis is measured-healthy, the carded reads are closed, and the only shape changes in-window are two user-approved rulesets I am measuring rather than opining on. Recs are one ledger close, one measurement gate, and the carried operational gap.

**Rec 1 (✅ ledger, no tuning axis) — close `tr61-rec1-gitignore-venv` as VERIFIED.**
Measured: `git ls-files .venv-ml` **0** (was 8,005); `.gitignore:12` = `.venv*/`; 10-20T22:30 backup **92 files → `717d196`** (was 8,098); tracked files **1,299** (was 9,221); `rev-list` `0	0`; `PUSH FAILED` 3 (unchanged). Record alongside it the caveat that `.git` is **906 MB** — history still holds the venv blobs, so **repo size is not fixed, only repo growth**; a history rewrite is a separate destructive decision not taken here.
`Verify:` `cd /Users/xsnyde2/polymarket-copybot && git ls-files .venv-ml | wc -l` → **0**; **AND** `grep "backed up" logs/cron/copybot-git-backup.log | tail -1` → a file count in the **40–200** range on each of the next 7 runs; **AND** `git rev-list --left-right --count origin/main...HEAD` → `0	0`.

**Rec 2 (🟡 NEW — measurement, no tuning axis) — pre-register the v63/v64 seven-day read for 2026-10-27.**
